<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

Hi protocol friends! I've been cooking up a Very Large PDS (vlpds) implementation capable of scaling up to billions of accounts and down to just one. It uses Object Storage as its only state, supports HA operation, live scaling up and down, is feature-complete with the reference PDS, and is FOSS.







<a href="https://vlpds.jazco.dev/docs/overview" class="bsky-link-preview-wrapper" target="_blank">
<div class="bsky-link-preview" markdown="1">

<div class="link-info" markdown="1">
**Overview · vlpds docs**
<p class="link-desc">An atproto PDS whose only durable storage is an object store. Any node serves any request, one node can run a personal server for free, and a few nodes can carry all of Bluesky's write load.</p>
<small>:material-link: https://vlpds.jazco.dev/docs/overview</small>
</div>
</div>
</a>

<div class="link-info" markdown="1">
**AIに実装を任せながら、Rustを学び続けたい。「Rust Learning Lab」を作りました - じゃあ、おうちで学べる **
<p class="link-desc">はじめに Rust Learning Labは、手元のRustコードを題材に、構文や型、所有権を説明してもらうためのCodex・Claude Code向けプラグインです。「この記号は何か」「なぜここで借用するのか」「この変更で何が変わるのか」といった疑問を、具体的なコードに沿って確かめられます。 github.com Rustを書き始めた人にも、コードは読めるけれど型や所有権を選んだ理由までは説明しきれない人にも使ってもらいたいと思っています。私が考えているのは、AIに何を任せ、何を自分で確かめるか、その判断を育てる機会を仕事の中にどう残すかということです。Rust Learning Labは…</p>
<small>:material-link: https://syu-m-5151.hatenablog.com/entry/2026/10...</small>

</div>
</a>

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/avatar/plain/did:plc:w76k4k66of3oz5jvtnqjv3wu/bafkreig7b6yr44x33bjo2zcrna5yyz4uopoqehdi2464qqc4at33lzvxym" class="bsky-avatar">

<br><small>2026-10-02</small>
</div>
</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

atproto in one breath: your account is yours, not the app's. Your data lives in a repo on a PDS, relays stream it out, and AppViews like Bluesky just present it. Feeds, labelers, and moderation are pluggable. Move servers, keep your identity. Open protocol, human-sized. 🌐







</div>
